- **walkthrough**: Create a concise, visual walkthrough of a branch, pull request, feature, code path, or architecture topic, with linked code evidence and manual test guidance. - **walkthrough**: Use this skill to produce a self-contained interactive HTML page that explains a concept, feature, changeset, commit, diff, branch, or PR.
